  hw.module private @picregs(in %clk : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>, in %reset : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>, in %we : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>, in %re : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>, in %bank : !hw.struct<value: i2, unknown: i2>, in %location : !hw.struct<value: i5, unknown: i5>, in %din : !hw.struct<value: i8, unknown: i8>, out dout : !hw.struct<value: i8, unknown: i8>) {

      
    %0 = sim.fmt.literal "Clearing SRAM.\0A"

      
    %1 = hw.aggregate_constant [0 : i32, -1 : i32] : !hw.struct<value: i32, unknown: i32>

      
    %2 = hw.aggregate_constant [false, true] : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>
